        (drop): Identity()
        (out_norm): Identity()
      )
      (presence_token_out_norm): LayerNorm((256,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
      (ref_point_head): MLP(
        (layers): ModuleList(
          (0): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=256, bias=True)
          (1): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
        )
        (drop): Identity()
        (out_norm): Identity()
      )
    )
  )
  (segmentation_head): UniversalSegmentationHead(
    (pixel_decoder): PixelDecoder(
      (conv_layers): ModuleList(
        (0-2): 3 x Conv2d(256, 256, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      )
      (norms): ModuleList(
        (0-2): 3 x GroupNorm(8, 256, eps=1e-05, affine=True)
      )
    )
    (mask_predictor): MaskPredictor(
      (mask_embed): MLP(
        (layers): ModuleList(
          (0-2): 3 x Linear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
        )
        (drop): Identity()
        (out_norm): Identity()
      )
    )
    (cross_attend_prompt): MultiheadAttentionWrapper(
      (out_proj): NonDynamicallyQuantizableLinear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
    )
    (cross_attn_norm): LayerNorm((256,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
    (instance_seg_head): Conv2d(256, 256, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  )
  (dot_prod_scoring): DotProductScoring(
    (prompt_mlp): MLP(
      (layers): ModuleList(
        (0): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=2048, bias=True)
        (1): Linear(in_features=2048, out_features=256, bias=True)
      )
      (drop): Dropout(p=0.1, inplace=False)
      (out_norm): LayerNorm((256,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
    )
    (prompt_proj): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
    (hs_proj): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
  )
  (matcher): BinaryHungarianMatcherV2(
    (norm): Sigmoid()
  )
)
